k < lnz; ++k) {\n        const size_t z = z0 + k;\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t gidx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx3(x, y, k + 1, nx, ny)] = (gidx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil on one local plane (interior x/y points only).\n// Boundary points are never touched: they were copied once at startup and\n// stay at their initial values, exactly as in the serial code where\n// output boundary = input boundary every iteration.\ninline void stencilPlane(const Real* __restrict__ input, Real* __restrict__ output,\n                         const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t plane) {\n    const size_t planeSize = nx * ny;\n    const Real* in = input + plane * planeSize;\n    Real* out = output + plane * planeSize;\n    for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n        const size_t row = y * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = row + x;\n            const Real center = in[idx];\n            const Real left = in[idx - 1];\n            const Real right = in[idx + 1];\n            const Real front = in[idx - nx];\n            const Real back = in[idx + nx];\n            const Real bottom = in[idx - planeSize];\n            const Real top = in[idx + planeSize];\n            out[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int Halo planes are contiguous in memory, so exchanges are single `MPI_Isend`/`MPI_Irecv` calls with no derived datatypes or packing.\n- **Communication/computation overlap**: each iteration posts the non-blocking halo exchange, computes all planes that don't touch the halos, then waits and finishes the two edge planes.\n- **Boundary optimization with identical semantics**: in the original, boundary cells are recopied from input every iteration, but since boundaries never change, they're seeded once before the timed loop — results are bit-identical.\n- **Grid initialization is fully distributed** (each rank computes its own slab from the global index formula), and the full grid is gathered to rank 0 with `MPI_Gatherv` only when `-r` or `-v` is requested, so `print_results`/validation output matches the serial code exactly.\n- **Robust edge cases**: more ranks than z-planes is handled via a sub-communicator of ranks that own planes; timing uses `MPI_Wtime` with a max-reduce across ranks.\n\n`stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt` adds `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX` — no new dependencies beyond MPI itself, which the parallelization requires.\n\n**Verification**: I built a serial reference from the original algorithm in /tmp and compared the full result output (Kahan sum + FNV hash) across 5 grid configurations (cubic, non-cubic, tiny nz=3/nz=5, odd iteration counts) × 5 rank counts (1–7).
